# Commentator ↔ host links: review list (updated 2026-10-24)

**623 links** between 2537 commentator claims (44 videos) and Candace Owens' archived claims: 173 contradict, 47 qualify, 403 support. Each pairs the commentator's claim (with its timestamped clip) with hers. Links record agreement or disagreement between speakers, not who is right.

🆕 marks the 99 links added on 2026-10-24 from the monitor-approved videos (Baron Coleman Ep 187–189, Paramount Tactical).

| Group | Contradicts | Qualifies | Supports |
|---|---|---|---|
| Charlie Kirk Show | 26 | 14 | 43 |
| Erika Kirk | 11 | 0 | 28 |
| Brian Harpole and Frank Turek | 96 | 14 | 101 |
| Baron Coleman | 11 | 14 | 213 |
| Paramount Tactical (Gary Melton) | 29 | 5 | 18 |

### Erika Kirk (11)
